# Unsafe Verification Ledger

Generated by `scripts/unsafe_census.py --ledger` from the machine census.
Do not hand-edit region rows; edit the artifact column in the sidecar
`docs/development/unsafe-verification-artifacts.tsv` (id -> artifact) and
regenerate. The gate (`scripts/unsafe_gate.py --check`) enforces that every
census id is accounted for here.

## Artifact taxonomy

| kind | meaning |
|------|---------|
| GAP | no verification artifact yet (accounted, unverified) |
| KANI:<harness> | Kani proof harness in kernel/verify (function name) |
| PROPTEST:<path> | proptest coverage in kernel/verify (file path) |
| LOOM:<test> | loom interleaving model (test name) |
| MIRI:<path> | covered by a Miri-executed mirror (file path) |
| INV:<id> | audited invariant (id from docs/architecture/*.md) |
